The fix makes the provider bypass the HA helper wrappers for set/delete credential writes and adds a UC-only capability fallback path.\n\n**Changes:**\n- Bypass `homeassistant.components.zwave_js.lock_helpers` for `async_set_credential` / `async_delete_credential` and call `node.access_control.{set_credential,delete_credential}` directly, mapping `SetCredentialResult` to LCM exceptions.\n- Enhance `async_get_capabilities` to detect UC-only locks (missing PIN or zero-slot PIN) and derive slot count via `zwave_js_server.util.lock.get_usercodes`, forcing slot-only routing.\n- Update/extend provider and e2e tests to cover UC-detection branches and the new direct-call write path.\n\n### Reviewed changes\n\nCopilot reviewed 4 out of 4 changed files in this pull request and generated 3 comments.\n\n| File | Description |\n| ---- | ----------- |\n| custom_components/lock_code_manager/providers/zwave_js.py | Implements UC-only capability fallback and direct `access_control` set/delete credential calls with status→exception mapping. |\n| tests/providers/zwave_js/test_provider.py | Adds UC-mode capability fallback tests and validates direct `access_control` primitive calls + status mapping. |\n| tests/providers/zwave_js/test_e2e.py | Updates E2E assertions to reflect the new user-then-credential primitive flow (user via helpers, credential via access_control). |\n| tests/providers/zwave_js/conftest.py | Extends the mocked `access_control` fixture to include write primitives returning `SetCredentialResult.OK` by default. |\n\n\n\n\n\n\n---\n\n💡 <a href=\"/raman325/lock_code_manager/new/main?filename=.github/instructions/*.instructions.md\" class=\"Link--inTextBlock\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\">Add ID: 3398948707
User: Copilot
File: custom_components/lock_code_manager/providers/zwave_js.py:270
Created: 2026-06-11T20:25:11Z
Review: 4480338310
In reply to: 
Body: The UC-fallback path currently swallows *all* exceptions from get_usercodes() and treats them as “no UC slots”. This can mis-detect capabilities during transient Z-Wave/driver failures (or hide programming errors), causing the integration to report no PIN support and route operations incorrectly. Prefer mapping Z-Wave transport errors to LockDisconnected and reachable/validation errors to LockOperationFailed, consistent with the rest of this provider.

---
ID: 3398948760
User: Copilot
File: custom_components/lock_code_manager/providers/zwave_js.py:439
Created: 2026-06-11T20:25:11Z
Review: 4480338310
In reply to: 
Body: async_set_credential now bypasses lock_helpers and calls node.access_control.set_credential directly, but it no longer catches HomeAssistantError. Other Z-Wave provider operations consistently wrap HomeAssistantError as LockOperationFailed; without this, a reachable-lock failure may bubble up as an unexpected exception and be treated as a bug by higher layers.

---
ID: 3398948780
User: Copilot
File: custom_components/lock_code_manager/providers/zwave_js.py:470
Created: 2026-06-11T20:25:11Z
Review: 4480338310
In reply to: 
Body: async_delete_credential now calls node.access_control.delete_credential directly, but it does not catch HomeAssistantError. For consistency with async_get_users/async_set_user/etc., reachable-lock failures should be wrapped as LockOperationFailed rather than bubbling up as unexpected exceptions.

---
ID: 3399418854
User: raman325
File: custom_components/lock_code_manager/providers/zwave_js.py:270
Created: 2026-06-11T21:56:10Z
Review: 4480891765
In reply to: 3398948707
Body: Addressed in a128f285 — dropped the blanket `except Exception` so any unexpected exception surfaces.

`get_usercodes()` is pure value-DB walking against `node.values` (a dict): it raises `NotFoundError` internally on missing slots (caught there, returns the accumulated list) and otherwise just returns. There's no transport call to fail transiently, so there's nothing realistic to map to `LockDisconnected`. A `KeyError`/`AttributeError` here would be a genuine bug, and the previous `except Exception` would have silently routed the entire lock to "no PIN support" — exactly the bad outcome you flagged.

---
ID: 3399419625
User: raman325
File: custom_components/lock_code_manager/providers/zwave_js.py:439
Created: 2026-06-11T21:56:22Z
Review: 4480892635
In reply to: 3398948760
Body: The `except HomeAssistantError` in other methods exists because they call `lock_helpers.*`, and the HA wrappers translate driver errors into `HomeAssistantError` subclasses with translation keys for the frontend. This direct call deliberately skips that wrapper to bypass its slot-range validation (the root cause of #1251).

`BaseZwaveJSServerError` is independent of `HomeAssistantError` — they're separate hierarchies — and `node.access_control.set_credential` is a pure zwave-js-server client call. There's no HA-layer wrapping in the path, so adding `except HomeAssistantError` here would be unreachable defensive code. Holding the line on this to keep the call surface honest.

---
ID: 3399419748
User: raman325
File: custom_components/lock_code_manager/providers/zwave_js.py:470
Created: 2026-06-11T21:56:24Z
Review: 4480892737
In reply to: 3398948780
Body: Same reasoning as #1252 (comment on line 449): `node.access_control.delete_credential` is a direct zwave-js-server client call, not an HA wrapper. The only thing it can raise is `BaseZwaveJSServerError` and subclasses (e.g. `FailedZWaveCommand`), which are independent of `HomeAssistantError`. The `HomeAssistantError` catch elsewhere in this provider is load-bearing only for `lock_helpers.*` callers.

---
ID: 3403073682
User: Copilot
File: custom_components/lock_code_manager/providers/_zwave_js_uc.py:224
Created: 2026-06-12T11:45:03Z
Review: 4485330884
In reply to: 
Body: _uc_slot_state() currently treats `in_use is None` the same as `False` because it uses `if not in_use:`. That means a slot with unknown `in_use` but a present/masked `usercode` will be incorrectly projected as empty (contradicting the docstring and the push-path behavior, which treats masked codes as unreadable even when `in_use` is unknown). Adjust the condition to only treat explicit `False` as empty, and treat `(in_use is None and usercode is None)` as empty.

---
ID: 3403073719
User: Copilot
File: custom_components/lock_code_manager/providers/_zwave_js_uc.py:352
Created: 2026-06-12T11:45:04Z
Review: 4485330884
In reply to: 
Body: _async_uc_clear_usercode() short-circuits on `not current.get(ATTR_IN_USE)`, which also evaluates true when `ATTR_IN_USE` is missing/unknown (None). That can incorrectly skip a clear when the cache is partially populated. Only treat the slot as already-clear when `ATTR_IN_USE` is explicitly `False`.
```

Here's a summary of all PR #1252 review comments:

## PR #1252 review comments

### Round 1 (2026-06-11, Copilot) — all 3 addressed in commit a128f285

1. **zwave_js.py:270** — `get_usercodes()` UC-fallback swallowing all exceptions. ✅ Addressed: dropped `except Exception`; you noted there's no transport path to map.
2. **zwave_js.py:439** — `async_set_credential` no longer catches `HomeAssistantError`. ✅ Replied: deliberate, direct `access_control` call bypasses HA wrapper, so the catch would be dead code.
3. **zwave_js.py:470** — same for `async_delete_credential`. ✅ Replied with same reasoning.

### Round 2 (2026-06-12, Copilot) — 2 NEW, not yet responded

Both concern `_zwave_js_uc.py` from the refactor (commit 7d24d415):

4. **`_zwave_js_uc.py:224`** — `_uc_slot_state()` uses `if not in_use:`, which conflates `None` (unknown) and `False`. Comment argues a slot with unknown `in_use` but a present masked `usercode` should not be projected as empty.
5. **`_zwave_js_uc.py:352`** — `_async_uc_clear_usercode()` short-circuits on `not current.get(ATTR_IN_USE)`, which also fires when the key is missing. Comment argues it should only skip the clear when `in_use is False` explicitly.

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
Both new comments are classic **truthy-vs-identity** bugs: Python's `not x` treats `None`, `False`, `0`, and `""` identically, but for tri-state Z-Wave value-DB attributes (`True` / `False` / unknown), those collapse two meaningful states into one. The push-path docstring you referenced ("treats masked codes as unreadable even when `in_use` is unknown") suggests the projection should distinguish them.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

Want me to (a) open the two flagged spots so you can decide, (b) draft a reply pushing back if you think the current behavior is intentional, or (c) make the fix + tests?

1	"""
2	Temporary User Code CC fallback support for the Z-Wave JS provider.
3	
4	Works around issue #1251: on some locks, node-zwave-js's unified
5	access-control API computes degenerate credential capabilities (PIN
6	missing or zero slots) from its cached interview data, and both Home
7	Assistant's ``lock_helpers`` and the driver itself validate every
8	credential write against that data -- bricking PIN management even
9	though the lock works fine through the legacy User Code CC. When the
10	node advertises User Code CC, this layer routes all PIN operations
11	through the legacy User Code CC value paths instead, restoring the
12	pre-4.0 behavior for exactly the population that needs it.
13	
14	The upstream fix is zwave-js/zwave-js#8873. Once the minimum supported
15	driver includes it, the unified API reports usable capabilities for
16	these locks, the detection in ``_uc_fallback_capabilities`` stops
17	triggering, and everything here goes dormant. To remove the fallback
18	entirely at that point:
19	
20	1. Delete this module.
21	2. Make ``ZWaveJSLock`` extend ``BaseLock`` directly again.
22	3. Delete the fallback branch points in ``zwave_js.py`` (grep for
23	   ``_uc_`` and ``_set_in_progress_code_slot``).
24	4. Delete ``tests/providers/zwave_js/test_uc_fallback.py`` and the
25	   ``uc_only_caps_response`` / ``uc_slot_walk`` / ``mock_uc_utils`` /
26	   ``uc_fallback_lock`` fixtures in that package's ``conftest.py``.
27	"""
28	
29	from __future__ import annotations
30	
31	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
32	import functools
33	import logging
34	from typing import Any, Literal
35	
36	from zwave_js_server.const import CommandClass, SetValueStatus
37	from zwave_js_server.const.command_class.lock import (
38	    ATTR_CODE_SLOT,
39	    ATTR_IN_USE,
40	    ATTR_USERCODE,
41	    LOCK_USERCODE_PROPERTY,
42	    LOCK_USERCODE_STATUS_PROPERTY,
43	    CodeSlotStatus,
44	)
45	from zwave_js_server.const.command_class.notification import (
46	    AccessControlNotificationEvent,
47	)
48	from zwave_js_server.exceptions import BaseZwaveJSServerError, NotFoundError
49	from zwave_js_server.model.node import Node
50	from zwave_js_server.util.lock import (
51	    clear_usercode,
52	    get_usercode,
53	    get_usercode_from_node,
54	    get_usercodes,
55	    set_usercode,
56	)
57	
58	from homeassistant.components.zwave_js.const import ATTR_EVENT
59	from homeassistant.core import Event, callback
60	
61	from ..domain.credentials import (
62	    Credential,
63	    CredentialType,
64	    CredentialTypeCapability,
65	    LockCapabilities,
66	    User,
67	)
68	from ..domain.exceptions import (
69	    CodeRejectedError,
70	    LockDisconnected,
71	    LockOperationFailed,
72	)
73	from ..domain.models import SlotCredential
74	from ._base import BaseLock
75	
76	_LOGGER = logging.getLogger(__name__)
77	
78	# SetValueResult statuses that mean a User Code CC value write was accepted.
79	_UC_SET_VALUE_OK = (
80	    SetValueStatus.SUCCESS,
81	    SetValueStatus.SUCCESS_UNSUPERVISED,
82	    SetValueStatus.WORKING,
83	)
84	
85	
86	@dataclass(repr=False, eq=False)
87	class ZWaveJSUserCodeFallbackSupport(BaseLock):
88	    """
89	    User Code CC fallback layer for the Z-Wave JS provider.
90	
91	    Holds every UC-fallback-specific field, helper, and push handler so
92	    the whole workaround can be deleted as a unit (see the module
93	    docstring for the removal recipe). The concrete provider keeps only
94	    the routing branch points that call into this layer.
95	    """
96	
97	    # Whether the unified access-control API is unusable for PIN management
98	    # on this lock and the legacy User Code CC utilities must be used
99	    # instead. None until ``async_get_capabilities`` runs the detection.
100	    # Deliberately stored (not re-probed per operation) so it shares the
101	    # lifetime of the base's capabilities cache: the seam's slot-only
102	    # routing decision is frozen from the same snapshot, and flipping one
103	    # without the other would route writes incoherently. Both reset
104	    # together when the provider is rebuilt (HA restart, LCM reload, or a
105	    # zwave_js entry reload -- which a driver upgrade always triggers),
106	    # so a lock healed by the upstream fix lands back in unified mode on
107	    # its next reload with no LCM change.
108	    _uc_fallback: bool | None = field(init=False, default=None)
109	    # Slot of a UC-fallback set operation currently in flight. User Code CC
110	    # has no in-band duplicate-rejection result; some firmwares report a
111	    # duplicate via an Access Control notification instead (sometimes with
112	    # userId=0). Tracking the in-flight slot lets the notification handler
113	    # attribute that rejection to the right slot.
114	    _set_in_progress_code_slot: int | None = field(init=False, default=None)
115	
116	    @property
117	    def node(self) -> Node:
118	        """Return the Z-Wave JS node; the concrete provider supplies this."""
119	        raise NotImplementedError
120	
121	    def _node_supports_user_code_cc(self) -> bool:
122	        """Return whether the node's endpoint 0 advertises User Code CC."""
123	        return any(cc.id == CommandClass.USER_CODE for cc in self.node.command_classes)
124	
125	    @functools.cached_property
126	    def _usercode_cc_version(self) -> int:
127	        """Return the User Code CC version supported by this node."""
128	        version = next(
129	            (
130	                cc.version
131	                for cc in self.node.command_classes
132	                if cc.id == CommandClass.USER_CODE
133	            ),
134	            0,
135	        )
136	        if version == 0:
137	            _LOGGER.warning(
138	                "Lock %s: User Code CC not found on node %s. This may "
139	                "indicate an incomplete interview. Defaulting to V1 behavior",
140	                self.lock.entity_id,
141	                self.node.node_id,
142	            )
143	            return 1
144	        return version
145	
146	    async def _async_uc_fallback_active(self) -> bool:
147	        """
148	        Return whether PIN operations must use the User Code CC fallback.
149	
150	        The flag is computed by ``async_get_capabilities``; when a
151	        credential operation arrives before any capability probe (e.g. a
152	        direct service call right after a reload), run the probe first so
153	        routing never guesses.
154	        """
155	        if self._uc_fallback is None:
156	            await self._get_cached_capabilities()
157	        return bool(self._uc_fallback)
158	
159	    def _uc_fallback_capabilities(self) -> LockCapabilities | None:
160	        """
161	        Detect the fallback and build slot-only capabilities for it.
162	
163	        Called by ``async_get_capabilities`` after the unified API
164	        reported no usable PIN support. Only falls back when the node
165	        advertises User Code CC -- without it the legacy utilities
166	        cannot work either, and the lock genuinely has no PIN support
167	        LCM can manage -- and when the User Code CC value DB walk finds
168	        slots. Sets ``_uc_fallback`` accordingly and returns None when
169	        no fallback is possible.
170	
171	        ``get_usercodes`` walks slot 1, 2, 3, ... in the value DB until
172	        ``NotFoundError``, so the returned list length is the lock's
173	        actual UC slot count. The function only raises ``NotFoundError``
174	        internally (caught there) and is otherwise pure value-DB
175	        walking, so we let any unexpected exception surface rather
176	        than silently mis-routing the lock to "no PIN support".
177	        """
178	        uc_slots = (
179	            get_usercodes(self.node) if self._node_supports_user_code_cc() else []
180	        )
181	        if not uc_slots:
182	            self._uc_fallback = False
183	            return None
184	        _LOGGER.warning(
185	            "Lock %s: unified access-control API reports no usable PIN "
186	            "capabilities but the node supports User Code CC with %s slots; "
187	            "falling back to legacy User Code CC handling (see issue #1251)",
188	            self.lock.entity_id,
189	            len(uc_slots),
190	        )
191	        self._uc_fallback = True
192	        return LockCapabilities(
193	            # Force slot-only routing: supports_user_management=False
194	            # gates _supports_user_records() at the seam, so the User
195	            # lifecycle (async_set_user / async_delete_user) is skipped
196	            # and our async_set_credential / async_delete_credential
197	            # become the direct call targets.
198	            supports_user_management=False,
199	            max_users=0,
200	            credential_types={
201	                CredentialType.PIN: CredentialTypeCapability(
202	                    num_slots=len(uc_slots),
203	                    # UC spec allows 4-10 ASCII digits per User Code CC v1+.
204	                    min_length=4,
205	                    max_length=10,
206	                    supports_learn=False,
207	                )
208	            },
209	            max_user_name_length=0,
210	        )
211	
212	    @staticmethod
213	    def _uc_slot_state(in_use: bool | None, usercode: str | None) -> SlotCredential:
214	        """
215	        Project a User Code CC slot to a ``SlotCredential``.
216	
217	        Masked codes (all asterisks) and occupied slots without a cached
218	        value count as unreadable; an unknown ``in_use`` (None) with no
219	        value counts as empty, matching the legacy 3.x reader.
220	        """
221	        if not in_use:
222	            return SlotCredential.empty()
223	        if not usercode:
224	            return SlotCredential.unreadable()
225	        code = str(usercode)
226	        if code == "*" * len(code):
227	            return SlotCredential.unreadable()
228	        return SlotCredential.known(code)
229	
230	    async def _async_uc_users_from_value_db(self) -> list[User]:
231	        """
232	        Synthesize one implicit user per occupied User Code CC slot.
233	
234	        User Code CC has no user records -- the user IS the credential --
235	        so each occupied slot becomes a user with ``user_id == slot``
236	        carrying its single PIN credential. The seam's slot projection
237	        and owner-resolution lookups (untagged user with ``user_id ==
238	        slot`` owning a PIN at ``credential.slot == slot``) match this
239	        shape via their legacy fallback path.
240	
241	        When any managed slot is missing from the value DB or has an
242	        unknown ``in_use`` state, do one hard refresh before projecting
243	        so a partially populated cache is not misread as empty slots.
244	        """
245	        try:
246	            slots = get_usercodes(self.node)
247	        except BaseZwaveJSServerError as err:
248	            raise LockDisconnected(f"get usercodes failed: {err}") from err
249	        slots_by_num = {int(slot[ATTR_CODE_SLOT]): slot for slot in slots}
250	        if any(
251	            slot_num not in slots_by_num
252	            or slots_by_num[slot_num].get(ATTR_IN_USE) is None
253	            for slot_num in self.managed_slots
254	        ):
255	            _LOGGER.debug(
256	                "Lock %s has missing/unknown slots, performing hard refresh",
257	                self.lock.entity_id,
258	            )
259	            await self._async_refresh_usercode_cache()
260	            try:
261	                slots = get_usercodes(self.node)
262	            except BaseZwaveJSServerError as err:
263	                raise LockDisconnected(f"get usercodes failed: {err}") from err
264	
265	        users: list[User] = []
266	        for slot_info in slots:
267	            slot = int(slot_info[ATTR_CODE_SLOT])
268	            state = self._uc_slot_state(
269	                slot_info.get(ATTR_IN_USE), slot_info.get(ATTR_USERCODE)
270	            )
271	            if state.is_empty:
272	                continue
273	            user = User(user_id=slot, name=None, active=True)
274	            user.credentials.append(
275	                Credential(type=CredentialType.PIN, slot=slot, state=state)
276	            )
277	            users.append(user)
278	        return users
279	
280	    async def _async_refresh_usercode_cache(self) -> None:
281	        """Refresh all User Code CC values from the device."""
282	        try:
283	            await self.node.async_refresh_cc_values(CommandClass.USER_CODE)
284	        except BaseZwaveJSServerError as err:
285	            raise LockDisconnected(f"usercode cache refresh failed: {err}") from err
286	
287	    async def _async_uc_set_usercode(self, code_slot: int, usercode: str) -> bool:
288	        """
289	        Write a usercode through the legacy User Code CC value path.
290	
291	        Returns False without writing when the cached value already
292	        matches (masked codes never match, so they are always
293	        rewritten). After a successful write, V1 locks are polled to
294	        force-update the value DB (they don't reliably report back),
295	        and the new state is pushed optimistically so the next sync
296	        tick doesn't read a stale cache and loop.
297	        """
298	        try:
299	            current = get_usercode(self.node, code_slot)
300	        except NotFoundError:
301	            current = None
302	        if current and current.get(ATTR_IN_USE):
303	            current_code = str(current.get(ATTR_USERCODE) or "")
304	            if current_code != "*" * len(current_code) and usercode == current_code:
305	                _LOGGER.debug(
306	                    "Lock %s slot %s already has this PIN, skipping set",
307	                    self.lock.entity_id,
308	                    code_slot,
309	                )
310	                return False
311	
312	        self._set_in_progress_code_slot = code_slot
313	        try:
314	            result = await set_usercode(self.node, code_slot, usercode)
315	        except NotFoundError as err:
316	            self._set_in_progress_code_slot = None
317	            raise CodeRejectedError(
318	                code_slot=code_slot,
319	                lock_entity_id=self.lock.entity_id,
320	                reason=f"slot not found on lock: {err}",
321	            ) from err
322	        except BaseZwaveJSServerError as err:
323	            self._set_in_progress_code_slot = None
324	            raise LockDisconnected(
325	                f"set usercode slot {code_slot} failed: {err}"
326	            ) from err
327	        if result is not None and result.status not in _UC_SET_VALUE_OK:
328	            self._set_in_progress_code_slot = None
329	            raise CodeRejectedError(
330	                code_slot=code_slot,
331	                lock_entity_id=self.lock.entity_id,
332	                reason=f"set value returned {result.status.name}",
333	            )
334	        await self._async_uc_verify_write(code_slot, "set")
335	        # Optimistic update: the value cache updates asynchronously via push
336	        # notification; push now to prevent sync loops from reading stale cache.
337	        self._push_credential_update(code_slot, SlotCredential.known(usercode))
338	        return True
339	
340	    async def _async_uc_clear_usercode(self, code_slot: int) -> bool:
341	        """
342	        Clear a usercode through the legacy User Code CC value path.
343	
344	        Returns False without writing when the slot is already clear.
345	        Mirrors ``_async_uc_set_usercode`` for verification and the
346	        optimistic push.
347	        """
348	        try:
349	            current = get_usercode(self.node, code_slot)
350	        except NotFoundError:
351	            current = None
352	        if current is not None and not current.get(ATTR_IN_USE):
353	            _LOGGER.debug(
354	                "Lock %s slot %s already cleared, skipping clear",
355	                self.lock.entity_id,
356	                code_slot,
357	            )
358	            return False
359	
360	        try:
361	            result = await clear_usercode(self.node, code_slot)
362	        except NotFoundError as err:
363	            raise LockOperationFailed(
364	                f"clear usercode slot {code_slot} failed: {err}"
365	            ) from err
366	        except BaseZwaveJSServerError as err:
367	            raise LockDisconnected(
368	                f"clear usercode slot {code_slot} failed: {err}"
369	            ) from err
370	        if result is not None and result.status not in _UC_SET_VALUE_OK:
371	            raise LockOperationFailed(
372	                f"clear usercode slot {code_slot} failed: "
373	                f"set value returned {result.status.name}"
374	            )
375	        await self._async_uc_verify_write(code_slot, "clear")
376	        # Optimistic update: see _async_uc_set_usercode for rationale.
377	        self._push_credential_update(code_slot, SlotCredential.empty())
378	        return True
379	
380	    async def _async_uc_verify_write(
381	        self, code_slot: int, operation: Literal["set", "clear"]
382	    ) -> None:
383	        """
384	        Force-update the value cache after a set/clear on a V1 lock.
385	
386	        V1 locks don't reliably update the Z-Wave JS value cache after a
387	        write. Poll the slot directly from the device to force-update the
388	        cache before the coordinator reads it, preventing sync loops.
389	        Wrap failures as LockDisconnected so they route to the retry path
390	        instead of suspending the slot.
391	        """
392	        if self._usercode_cc_version >= 2:
393	            return
394	        try:
395	            await get_usercode_from_node(self.node, code_slot)
396	        except BaseZwaveJSServerError as err:
397	            raise LockDisconnected(
398	                f"Post-{operation} verification poll failed for "
399	                f"{self.lock.entity_id} slot {code_slot}: {err}"
400	            ) from err
401	
402	    def _uc_code_slot_in_use(self, code_slot: int) -> bool | None:
403	        """Return whether a User Code CC slot is in use, None when unknown."""
404	        try:
405	            return get_usercode(self.node, code_slot)[ATTR_IN_USE]
406	        except NotFoundError, KeyError:
407	            return None
408	
409	    @callback
410	    def _on_uc_value_updated(self, event: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
411	        """Handle ``value updated`` node events for User Code CC values."""
412	        args: dict[str, Any] = event["args"]
413	        if args.get("commandClass") != CommandClass.USER_CODE:
414	            return
415	
416	        property_name = args.get("property")
417	        if property_name not in (
418	            LOCK_USERCODE_PROPERTY,
419	            LOCK_USERCODE_STATUS_PROPERTY,
420	        ):
421	            return
422	
423	        code_slot = int(args["propertyKey"])
424	        # Slot 0 is not a valid user code slot.
425	        if code_slot == 0:
426	            return
427	
428	        # Clear in-progress tracking only on userCode updates for the slot
429	        # we were setting. userIdStatus updates don't confirm acceptance and
430	        # could race with duplicate-code notifications.
431	        if (
432	            property_name == LOCK_USERCODE_PROPERTY
433	            and code_slot == self._set_in_progress_code_slot
434	        ):
435	            self._set_in_progress_code_slot = None
436	
437	        if property_name == LOCK_USERCODE_STATUS_PROPERTY:
438	            self._handle_uc_status_update(code_slot, args.get("newValue"))
439	        else:
440	            self._handle_uc_value_update(code_slot, args.get("newValue"))
441	
442	    @callback
443	    def _handle_uc_status_update(self, code_slot: int, status: Any) -> None:
444	        """Handle a userIdStatus value update for a code slot."""
445	        if status == CodeSlotStatus.AVAILABLE:
446	            # Ignore AVAILABLE status if Lock Code Manager expects a PIN on this
447	            # slot. Some locks send stale AVAILABLE events after a code was set,
448	            # which would cause infinite sync loops.
449	            if (
450	                self.coordinator
451	                and self.coordinator.desired_credential(code_slot).is_present
452	            ):
453	                _LOGGER.debug(
454	                    "Lock %s: ignoring userIdStatus=AVAILABLE for slot %s "
455	                    "(LCM expects PIN on this slot)",
456	                    self.lock.entity_id,
457	                    code_slot,
458	                )
459	                return
460	
461	            # Slot was cleared - update coordinator if needed
462	            current = self.coordinator.data.get(code_slot) if self.coordinator else None
463	            if self.coordinator and (current is None or not current.is_empty):
464	                _LOGGER.debug(
465	                    "Lock %s: slot %s userIdStatus=AVAILABLE, marking cleared",
466	                    self.lock.entity_id,
467	                    code_slot,
468	                )
469	                self._push_credential_update(code_slot, SlotCredential.empty())
470	
471	    @callback
472	    def _handle_uc_value_update(self, code_slot: int, new_value: Any) -> None:
473	        """Handle a userCode value update for a code slot."""
474	        if not new_value:
475	            resolved = SlotCredential.empty()
476	        else:
477	            value = str(new_value)
478	            slot_in_use = self._uc_code_slot_in_use(code_slot)
479	            # Asymmetric in_use checks: masked codes count as unreadable even
480	            # when in_use is None (some firmwares mask before reporting
481	            # status), but all-zeros only counts as empty when in_use is
482	            # explicitly False (zeros from a partially-loaded cache must
483	            # not be misread as cleared).
484	            if value == "*" * len(value) and slot_in_use is not False:
485	                resolved = SlotCredential.unreadable()
486	            elif value.strip("0") == "" and slot_in_use is False:
487	                resolved = SlotCredential.empty()
488	            else:
489	                resolved = SlotCredential.known(value)
490	
491	        # Z-Wave JS sends duplicate events; skip if the value is unchanged.
492	        if self.coordinator and self.coordinator.data.get(code_slot) == resolved:
493	            return
494	
495	        _LOGGER.debug(
496	            "Lock %s received push update for slot %s: %s",
497	            self.lock.entity_id,
498	            code_slot,
499	            "****" if resolved.is_readable else f"({resolved.as_label()})",
500	        )
501	        self._push_credential_update(code_slot, resolved)
502	
503	    @callback
504	    def _uc_handle_duplicate_notification(self, evt: Event, code_slot: int) -> bool:
505	        """
506	        Attribute a duplicate-code notification to the in-flight set, if any.
507	
508	        Only fires when LCM initiated the set (the in-progress slot is
509	        only tracked by the UC-fallback write path; unified-mode writes
510	        report duplicates in-band). Marks the slot as rejected so the
511	        sync manager raises DuplicateCodeError on the next tick, routing
512	        through the standard CodeRejectedError flow. Some Z-Wave lock
513	        firmwares report this notification with userId=0 instead of the
514	        offending slot; treat 0 as referring to the slot we're currently
515	        setting. Returns True when the notification was consumed.
516	        """
517	        if (
518	            evt.data[ATTR_EVENT]
519	            == AccessControlNotificationEvent.NEW_USER_CODE_NOT_ADDED_DUE_TO_DUPLICATE_CODE
520	            and self._set_in_progress_code_slot is not None
521	            and code_slot in (0, self._set_in_progress_code_slot)
522	        ):
523	            slot = self._set_in_progress_code_slot
524	            self._set_in_progress_code_slot = None
525	            self.mark_code_rejected(slot)
526	            return True
527	        return False
528	
```

> AGENT

Both Copilot comments are legitimate. Let me confirm the reasoning against the actual code.

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
The whole UC fallback layer treats `in_use` as a **tri-state** (True / False / None) — and gets that right in some places but not others. The push-path handler at lines 478-489 even spells out the asymmetric rule in a comment: "masked codes count as unreadable even when in_use is None ... all-zeros only counts as empty when in_use is explicitly False." That's the contract; the two flagged sites violate it.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

## Comment 1: `_uc_slot_state` (line 221) — **legitimate**

Code path with `in_use=None, usercode="****"`:
- Current: `if not in_use:` → returns `SlotCredential.empty()`
- Push-path equivalent: matches `value == "*" * len(value) and slot_in_use is not False` → `unreadable`
- The docstring itself says: "Masked codes (all asterisks) ... count as unreadable" — universal, not conditioned on `in_use`.

So the function contradicts (a) its own docstring and (b) the push-path's explicit rule. The hard refresh in `_async_uc_users_from_value_db` mitigates it for *managed* slots but doesn't help unmanaged ones, and even the refresh can leave `in_use=None`.

## Comment 2: `_async_uc_clear_usercode` (line 352) — **legitimate**

`not current.get(ATTR_IN_USE)` fires for `False`, `None`, *and* the key being absent. Compare to the SET short-circuit five lines up:

```python
if current and current.get(ATTR_IN_USE):          # SET: truthy → True only
```
vs.
```python
if current is not None and not current.get(ATTR_IN_USE):   # CLEAR: falsy → False, None, missing
```

Asymmetric and unsafe in the partial-cache case: skipping a clear leaves a live PIN on the lock; redoing an already-empty clear is a harmless no-op. The conservative choice is `is False`.

## Tests don't cover these cases

> AGENT

Neither flagged case is covered. Tests at lines 854/885/916 use `in_use=None` only to trigger the refresh path, then immediately mutate it back to definite values before `_uc_slot_state` reads them.

## Verdict

Both comments are legitimate, both expose tri-state-vs-truthiness bugs, and neither has test coverage. The fixes are small:

```python
# _uc_slot_state — match the push-path contract
if in_use is False:
    return SlotCredential.empty()
if not usercode:
    return SlotCredential.empty() if in_use is None else SlotCredential.unreadable()
code = str(usercode)
if code == "*" * len(code):
    return SlotCredential.unreadable()
return SlotCredential.known(code)

# _async_uc_clear_usercode — explicit False only
if current is not None and current.get(ATTR_IN_USE) is False:
    ...
    return False
```

The blast radius is genuinely small (after the hard refresh, `in_use` is usually definite), but they're real correctness gaps and they contradict the push-path's stated contract in code comments. Worth fixing in this PR while the layer is still under review.

Want me to apply both fixes plus regression tests for the four uncovered states (`in_use=None`, `usercode=masked`), then push and reply on the thread?

60	    EMPTY = "empty"
61	    UNREADABLE_CODE = "unreadable_code"
62	
63	
64	@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
65	class SlotCredential:
66	    """
67	    Credential state for one slot on one lock.
68	
69	    Three constructors:
70	    - ``empty()`` -- slot is cleared on the lock
71	    - ``unreadable()`` -- slot holds a code whose value is write-only
72	    - ``known(pin)`` -- slot holds a code whose value the provider exposes
73	
74	    Treat as opaque; consume via accessors not direct field access.
75	    """
76	
77	    present: bool
78	    pin: str | None
79	
80	    @classmethod
81	    def empty(cls) -> SlotCredential:
82	        """Return the shared "slot is cleared" credential."""
83	        return _EMPTY_CREDENTIAL
84	
85	    @classmethod
86	    def unreadable(cls) -> SlotCredential:
87	        """Return the shared "slot holds a write-only code" credential."""
88	        return _UNREADABLE_CREDENTIAL
89	
90	    @classmethod
91	    def known(cls, pin: str) -> SlotCredential:
92	        """Return a credential carrying a readable PIN."""
93	        return cls(present=True, pin=pin)
94	
95	    @property
96	    def is_empty(self) -> bool:
97	        """Return True when the slot holds no code."""
98	        return not self.present
99	
100	    @property
101	    def is_present(self) -> bool:
102	        """Return True when the slot holds a code."""
103	        return self.present
104	
105	    @property
106	    def is_readable(self) -> bool:
107	        """Return True when the credential exposes a comparable PIN."""
108	        return self.present and self.pin is not None
109	
110	    @property
111	    def readable_pin(self) -> str | None:
112	        """Return the PIN when readable, otherwise ``None``."""
113	        return self.pin if self.is_readable else None
114	
115	    def matches(self, pin: str) -> bool:
116	        """Return True when this credential is readable and equals ``pin``."""
117	        return self.is_readable and self.pin == pin
118	
119	    def as_label(self) -> str | SlotCode:
120	        """Return stable serialization for diagnostics/websocket consumers."""
121	        if not self.present:
122	            return SlotCode.EMPTY
123	        if self.pin is None:
124	            return SlotCode.UNREADABLE_CODE
125	        return self.pin
126	
127	
128	_EMPTY_CREDENTIAL: Final = SlotCredential(present=False, pin=None)
129	_UNREADABLE_CREDENTIAL: Final = SlotCredential(present=True, pin=None)
130	
131	
132	@dataclass
133	class LockCodeManagerConfigEntryRuntimeData:
134	    """Runtime data for a Lock Code Manager config entry."""

  "old_string": "    @staticmethod\n    def _uc_slot_state(in_use: bool | None, usercode: str | None) -> SlotCredential:\n        \"\"\"\n        Project a User Code CC slot to a ``SlotCredential``.\n\n        Masked codes (all asterisks) and occupied slots without a cached\n        value count as unreadable; an unknown ``in_use`` (None) with no\n        value counts as empty, matching the legacy 3.x reader.\n        \"\"\"\n        if not in_use:\n            return SlotCredential.empty()\n        if not usercode:\n            return SlotCredential.unreadable()\n        code = str(usercode)\n        if code == \"*\" * len(code):\n            return SlotCredential.unreadable()\n        return SlotCredential.known(code)",
  "new_string": "    @staticmethod\n    def _uc_slot_state(in_use: bool | None, usercode: str | None) -> SlotCredential:\n        \"\"\"\n        Project a User Code CC slot to a ``SlotCredential``.\n\n        Slots count as empty only when ``in_use`` is explicitly ``False``,\n        or when ``in_use`` is unknown (``None``) with no cached value --\n        the latter matches the legacy 3.x reader. Masked codes (all\n        asterisks) and occupied slots without a cached value count as\n        unreadable; an unknown ``in_use`` with a present value is treated\n        as occupied so a partially populated cache cannot erase a live\n        PIN (mirrors the push-path rule at ``_handle_uc_value_update``).\n        \"\"\"\n        if in_use is False:\n            return SlotCredential.empty()\n        if not usercode:\n            return (\n                SlotCredential.empty()\n                if in_use is None\n                else SlotCredential.unreadable()\n            )\n        code = str(usercode)\n        if code == \"*\" * len(code):\n            return SlotCredential.unreadable()\n        return SlotCredential.known(code)"
}

> AGENT

Pushed `1160f4b0` to PR #1252.

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
The fix mirrors a pattern that was already explicitly spelled out a hundred lines later in `_handle_uc_value_update`: "masked codes count as unreadable even when `in_use` is None ... all-zeros only counts as empty when `in_use` is explicitly False." That comment was the contract; `_uc_slot_state` and `_async_uc_clear_usercode` just hadn't been brought into line with it. Worth treating that comment as a checklist next time you touch any path that reads `in_use`.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

## Summary

- **`_uc_slot_state`**: `in_use is False` → empty; `not usercode` with `in_use=None` → empty (matches legacy 3.x reader), else unreadable; masked → unreadable regardless of `in_use`.
- **`_async_uc_clear_usercode`**: short-circuit only when `current.get(ATTR_IN_USE) is False`.
- **Tests**: 8-case parametrized `_uc_slot_state` projection table + 2-case parametrized proceed-when-`in_use`-unknown clear test. 10 new tests, all 52 file tests pass; full suite 1223 pass.

61	_LOGGER = logging.getLogger(__name__)
62	
63	# All known Access Control Notification CC events that indicate the lock is locked
64	# or unlocked
65	ACCESS_CONTROL_NOTIFICATION_TO_LOCKED = {
66	    True: (
67	        AccessControlNotificationEvent.AUTO_LOCK_LOCKED_OPERATION,
68	        AccessControlNotificationEvent.KEYPAD_LOCK_OPERATION,
69	        AccessControlNotificationEvent.LOCK_OPERATION_WITH_USER_CODE,
70	        AccessControlNotificationEvent.LOCKED_BY_RF_WITH_INVALID_USER_CODE,
71	        AccessControlNotificationEvent.MANUAL_LOCK_OPERATION,
72	        AccessControlNotificationEvent.RF_LOCK_OPERATION,
73	    ),
74	    False: (
75	        AccessControlNotificationEvent.KEYPAD_UNLOCK_OPERATION,
76	        AccessControlNotificationEvent.MANUAL_UNLOCK_OPERATION,
77	        AccessControlNotificationEvent.RF_UNLOCK_OPERATION,
78	        AccessControlNotificationEvent.UNLOCK_BY_RF_WITH_INVALID_USER_CODE,
79	        AccessControlNotificationEvent.UNLOCK_OPERATION_WITH_USER_CODE,
80	    ),
81	}
82	
83	
84	@dataclass(repr=False, eq=False)
85	class ZWaveJSLock(BaseLock):
86	    """Class to represent ZWave JS lock."""
87	
88	    lock_config_entry: ConfigEntry = field(repr=False)
89	    # Home Assistant event-bus listeners (separate lifecycle from push
90	    # subscriptions: registered in ``async_setup``, released in
91	    # ``async_unload``).
92	    _listeners: list[Callable[[], None]] = field(init=False, default_factory=list)
93	    _set_in_progress_code_slot: int | None = field(init=False, default=None)
94	
95	    @property
96	    def node(self) -> Node:
97	        """Return ZWave JS node."""
98	        return async_get_node_from_entity_id(
99	            self.hass, self.lock.entity_id, self.ent_reg
100	        )
101	
102	    @functools.cached_property
103	    def _usercode_cc_version(self) -> int:
104	        """Return the User Code CC version supported by this node."""
105	        version = next(
106	            (
107	                cc.version
108	                for cc in self.node.command_classes
109	                if cc.id == CommandClass.USER_CODE
110	            ),
111	            0,
112	        )
113	        if version == 0:
114	            _LOGGER.warning(
115	                "Lock %s: User Code CC not found on node %s. This may "
116	                "indicate an incomplete interview. Defaulting to V1 behavior",
117	                self.lock.entity_id,
118	                self.node.node_id,
119	            )
120	            return 1
121	        return version
122	
123	    @property
124	    def supports_push(self) -> bool:
125	        """Return whether this lock supports push-based updates."""
126	        return True
127	
128	    @property
129	    def connection_check_interval(self) -> timedelta | None:
130	        """Z-Wave JS exposes config entry state changes, so skip polling."""
131	        return None
132	
133	    def _get_client_state(self) -> tuple[bool, str]:
134	        """Return whether the Z-Wave JS client is ready and a retry reason."""
135	        if self.lock_config_entry.state != ConfigEntryState.LOADED:
136	            return False, "config entry not loaded"
137	
138	        runtime_data: ZwaveJSData | None = getattr(
139	            self.lock_config_entry, "runtime_data", None
140	        )
141	        client: Client | None = (
142	            getattr(runtime_data, "client", None) if runtime_data else None
143	        )
144	        if not client:
145	            return False, "Z-Wave JS client not ready"
146	
147	        if not client.connected:
148	            return False, "Z-Wave JS client not connected"
149	
150	        if client.driver is None:
151	            return False, "Z-Wave JS driver not ready"
152	
153	        return True, ""
154	
155	    def code_slot_in_use(self, code_slot: int) -> bool | None:
156	        """Return whether a code slot is in use."""
157	        try:
158	            return get_usercode(self.node, code_slot)[ATTR_IN_USE]
159	        except KeyError, ValueError:
160	            return None
161	
162	    @callback
163	    def _handle_usercode_status_update(self, code_slot: int, status: Any) -> None:
164	        """Handle userIdStatus value update for a code slot."""
165	        if status == CodeSlotStatus.AVAILABLE:
166	            # Ignore AVAILABLE status if Lock Code Manager expects a PIN on this
167	            # slot. Some locks send stale AVAILABLE events after a code was set,
168	            # which would cause infinite sync loops.
169	            if (
170	                self.coordinator
171	                and self.coordinator.desired_credential(code_slot).is_present
172	            ):
173	                _LOGGER.debug(
174	                    "Lock %s: ignoring userIdStatus=AVAILABLE for slot %s "
175	                    "(LCM expects PIN on this slot)",
176	                    self.lock.entity_id,
177	                    code_slot,
178	                )
179	                return
180	
181	            # Slot was cleared - update coordinator if needed
182	            current = self.coordinator.data.get(code_slot) if self.coordinator else None
183	            if self.coordinator and (current is None or not current.is_empty):
184	                _LOGGER.debug(
185	                    "Lock %s: slot %s userIdStatus=AVAILABLE, marking cleared",
186	                    self.lock.entity_id,
187	                    code_slot,
188	                )
189	                self._push_credential_update(code_slot, SlotCredential.empty())
190	
191	    @callback
192	    def _handle_usercode_value_update(self, code_slot: int, new_value: Any) -> None:
193	        """Handle userCode value update for a code slot."""
194	        if not new_value:
195	            resolved = SlotCredential.empty()
196	        else:
197	            value = str(new_value)
198	            slot_in_use = self.code_slot_in_use(code_slot)
199	            # Asymmetric in_use checks: masked codes count as unreadable even
200	            # when in_use is None (some firmwares mask before reporting
201	            # status), but all-zeros only counts as empty when in_use is
202	            # explicitly False (zeros from a partially-loaded cache must
203	            # not be misread as cleared).
204	            if value == "*" * len(value) and slot_in_use is not False:
205	                resolved = SlotCredential.unreadable()
206	            elif value.strip("0") == "" and slot_in_use is False:
207	                resolved = SlotCredential.empty()
208	            else:
209	                resolved = SlotCredential.known(value)
210	
211	        # Z-Wave JS sends duplicate events; skip if the value is unchanged.
212	        if self.coordinator and self.coordinator.data.get(code_slot) == resolved:
213	            return
214	
215	        _LOGGER.debug(
216	            "Lock %s received push update for slot %s: %s",
217	            self.lock.entity_id,
218	            code_slot,
219	            "****" if resolved.is_readable else f"({resolved.as_label()})",
220	        )
221	        self._push_credential_update(code_slot, resolved)
222	
223	    @callback
224	    def setup_push_subscription(self) -> None:
225	        """Subscribe to User Code CC value update events."""
226	        if self._push_unsubs:
227	            return
228	
229	        ready, reason = self._get_client_state()
230	        if not ready:
231	            raise LockDisconnected(reason)
232	
233	        @callback
234	        def on_value_updated(event: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
235	            """Handle value update events from Z-Wave JS."""
236	            args: dict[str, Any] = event["args"]
237	            if args.get("commandClass") != CommandClass.USER_CODE:
238	                return
239	
240	            property_name = args.get("property")
241	            if property_name not in (
242	                LOCK_USERCODE_PROPERTY,
243	                LOCK_USERCODE_STATUS_PROPERTY,
244	            ):
245	                return
246	
247	            code_slot = int(args["propertyKey"])
248	
249	            # Slot 0 is not a valid user code slot.
250	            if code_slot == 0:
251	                return
252	
253	            # Clear in-progress tracking only on userCode updates for the slot
254	            # we were setting. userIdStatus updates don't confirm acceptance and
255	            # could race with duplicate-code notifications.
256	            if (
257	                property_name == LOCK_USERCODE_PROPERTY
258	                and code_slot == self._set_in_progress_code_slot
259	            ):
260	                self._set_in_progress_code_slot = None
261	
262	            if property_name == LOCK_USERCODE_STATUS_PROPERTY:
263	                self._handle_usercode_status_update(code_slot, args.get("newValue"))
264	            else:
265	                self._handle_usercode_value_update(code_slot, args.get("newValue"))
266	
267	        try:
268	            unsub = self.node.on("value updated", on_value_updated)
269	        except ValueError as err:
270	            raise LockDisconnected(f"node not ready: {err}") from err
271	        self._register_push_unsub(unsub)
272	
273	    @callback
274	    def teardown_push_subscription(self) -> None:
275	        """Unsubscribe from value update events."""
276	        self._clear_push_unsubs()
277	
278	    @callback
279	    def _zwave_js_event_filter(self, event_data: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
280	        """Return True if the event belongs to this lock's node."""
281	        assert self.node.client.driver
282	        return (
283	            event_data[ATTR_HOME_ID] == self.node.client.driver.controller.home_id
284	            and event_data[ATTR_NODE_ID] == self.node.node_id
285	            and event_data[ATTR_DEVICE_ID] == self.lock.device_id
286	        )
287	
288	    @callback
289	    def _handle_zwave_js_event(self, evt: Event) -> None:
290	        """Handle Z-Wave JS event."""
291	        if evt.data[ATTR_TYPE] != NotificationType.ACCESS_CONTROL:
292	            _LOGGER.debug(
293	                "Lock %s received non Access Control event: %s",
294	                self.lock.entity_id,
295	                evt.as_dict(),
296	            )
297	            return
298	
299	        params = evt.data.get(ATTR_PARAMETERS) or {}
300	        code_slot = params.get("userId", 0)
301	
302	        # Handle duplicate code rejection — only when LCM initiated the set.
303	        # Mark the slot as rejected so the sync manager raises DuplicateCodeError
304	        # on the next tick, routing through the standard CodeRejectedError flow
305	        # (tracker reset, circuit breaker awareness, notification).
306	        # Some Z-Wave lock firmwares report this notification with userId=0
307	        # instead of the offending slot; treat 0 as referring to the slot
308	        # we're currently setting.
309	        if (
310	            evt.data[ATTR_EVENT]
311	            == AccessControlNotificationEvent.NEW_USER_CODE_NOT_ADDED_DUE_TO_DUPLICATE_CODE
312	            and self._set_in_progress_code_slot is not None
313	            and code_slot in (0, self._set_in_progress_code_slot)
314	        ):
315	            slot = self._set_in_progress_code_slot
316	            self._set_in_progress_code_slot = None
317	            self.mark_code_rejected(slot)
318	            return
319	
320	        self.async_fire_code_slot_event(
321	            code_slot=code_slot,
322	            to_locked=next(
323	                (
324	                    to_locked
325	                    for to_locked, codes in ACCESS_CONTROL_NOTIFICATION_TO_LOCKED.items()
326	                    if evt.data[ATTR_EVENT] in codes
327	                ),
328	                None,
329	            ),
330	            action_text=evt.data.get(ATTR_EVENT_LABEL),
331	            source_data=evt,
332	        )
333	
334	    @property
335	    def domain(self) -> str:
336	        """Return integration domain."""
337	        return ZWAVE_JS_DOMAIN
338	
339	    def _clear_listeners(self) -> None:
340	        """Unsubscribe and clear all HA event bus listeners."""
341	        for listener in self._listeners:
342	            listener()
343	        self._listeners.clear()
344	
345	    async def async_setup(self, config_entry: ConfigEntry) -> None:
346	        """
347	        Set up lock by provider.
348	
349	        Idempotent: clears existing listeners before re-registering.
350	        """
351	        self._clear_listeners()
352	        self._listeners.append(
353	            self.hass.bus.async_listen(
354	                ZWAVE_JS_NOTIFICATION_EVENT,
355	                self._handle_zwave_js_event,
356	                self._zwave_js_event_filter,
357	            )
358	        )
359	
360	    async def async_unload(self, remove_permanently: bool) -> None:
361	        """Unload lock."""
362	        self._clear_listeners()
363	        await super().async_unload(remove_permanently)
364	
365	    async def async_is_integration_connected(self) -> bool:
366	        """Return whether the Z-Wave JS client is connected."""
367	        ready, _reason = self._get_client_state()
368	        return ready
369	
370	    async def async_is_device_available(self) -> bool:
371	        """Return whether the Z-Wave node is available for commands."""
372	        try:
373	            return self.node.status != NodeStatus.DEAD
374	        except Exception as err:
375	            _LOGGER.debug(
376	                "Lock %s: failed to check device availability: %s",
377	                self.lock.entity_id,
378	                err,
379	            )
380	            return False
381	
382	    async def async_hard_refresh_codes(self) -> dict[int, SlotCredential]:
383	        """Refresh the User Code CC cache from the device and return all codes."""
384	        await self._async_refresh_usercode_cache()
385	        return await self.async_get_usercodes()
386	
387	    async def _async_verify_write(
388	        self, code_slot: int, operation: Literal["set", "clear"]
389	    ) -> None:
390	        """
391	        Force-update the value cache after a set/clear on a V1 lock.
392	
393	        V1 locks don't reliably update the Z-Wave JS value cache after a write.
394	        Poll the slot directly from the device to force-update the cache before
395	        the coordinator reads it, preventing sync loops. Wrap failures as
396	        LockDisconnected so they route to the retry path instead of leaking a
397	        raw FailedZWaveCommand into the generic exception handler, which would
398	        otherwise suspend the lock.
399	        """
400	        if self._usercode_cc_version >= 2:
401	            return
402	        try:
403	            await get_usercode_from_node(self.node, code_slot)
404	        except FailedZWaveCommand as err:
405	            raise LockDisconnected(
406	                f"Post-{operation} verification poll failed for "
407	                f"{self.lock.entity_id} slot {code_slot}: {err}"
408	            ) from err
409	
410	    async def async_set_usercode(
411	        self,
412	        code_slot: int,
413	        usercode: str,
414	        name: str | None = None,
415	        source: Literal["sync", "direct"] = "direct",
416	    ) -> bool:
417	        """
418	        Set a usercode on a code slot.
419	
420	        Returns True if the value was changed, False if already set to this value.
421	        """
422	        # Cache lookup short-circuits no-op writes. Bare-except is intentional:
423	        # a stale or missing cache entry must not block the set operation.
424	        try:
425	            if (current := get_usercode(self.node, code_slot)).get("in_use"):
426	                current_code = str(current.get("usercode", ""))
427	                # Skip the duplicate check if the current code is masked.
428	                if current_code != "*" * len(current_code) and usercode == current_code:
429	                    _LOGGER.debug(
430	                        "Lock %s slot %s already has this PIN, skipping set",
431	                        self.lock.entity_id,
432	                        code_slot,
433	                    )
434	                    return False
435	        except Exception:
436	            pass
437	
438	        self._set_in_progress_code_slot = code_slot
439	        service_data = {
440	            ATTR_ENTITY_ID: self.lock.entity_id,
441	            ATTR_CODE_SLOT: code_slot,
442	            ATTR_USERCODE: usercode,
443	        }
444	        await self.async_call_service(
445	            ZWAVE_JS_DOMAIN, SERVICE_SET_LOCK_USERCODE, service_data
446	        )
447	        await self._async_verify_write(code_slot, "set")
448	        # Optimistic update: the value cache updates asynchronously via push
449	        # notification; push now to prevent sync loops from reading stale cache.
450	        self._push_credential_update(code_slot, SlotCredential.known(usercode))
451	        return True
452	
453	    async def async_clear_usercode(self, code_slot: int) -> bool:
454	        """
455	        Clear a usercode on a code slot.
456	
457	        Returns True if the value was changed, False if already cleared.
458	        """
459	        # Cache lookup short-circuits no-op clears. Bare-except is intentional:
460	        # see async_set_usercode for rationale.
461	        try:
462	            current = get_usercode(self.node, code_slot)
463	            if not current.get("in_use"):
464	                _LOGGER.debug(
465	                    "Lock %s slot %s already cleared, skipping clear",
466	                    self.lock.entity_id,
467	                    code_slot,
468	                )
469	                return False
470	        except Exception:
471	            pass
472	
473	        service_data = {
474	            ATTR_ENTITY_ID: self.lock.entity_id,
475	            ATTR_CODE_SLOT: code_slot,
476	        }
477	        await self.async_call_service(
478	            ZWAVE_JS_DOMAIN, SERVICE_CLEAR_LOCK_USERCODE, service_data
479	        )
480	        await self._async_verify_write(code_slot, "clear")
481	        # Optimistic update: see async_set_usercode for rationale.
482	        self._push_credential_update(code_slot, SlotCredential.empty())
483	        return True
484	
485	    def _get_usercodes_from_cache(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
486	        """Get usercodes from Z-Wave JS value DB cache."""
487	        try:
488	            return list(get_usercodes(self.node) or [])
489	        except Exception as err:
490	            raise LockDisconnected from err
491	
492	    async def _async_refresh_usercode_cache(self) -> None:
493	        """Refresh usercode cache from the device."""
494	        try:
495	            await self.node.async_refresh_cc_values(CommandClass.USER_CODE)
496	        except Exception as err:
497	            raise LockDisconnected from err
498	
499	    async def async_get_usercodes(self) -> dict[int, SlotCredential]:
500	        """Get dictionary of code slots and usercodes."""
501	        code_slots = self.managed_slots
502	        data: dict[int, SlotCredential] = {}
503	
504	        if not await self.async_is_integration_connected():
505	            raise LockDisconnected
506	
507	        slots = self._get_usercodes_from_cache()
508	        slots_by_num = {int(slot["code_slot"]): slot for slot in slots}
509	
510	        # If any managed slot is missing or has unknown in_use state, do one hard
511	        # refresh. Call _async_refresh_usercode_cache directly (not
512	        # async_hard_refresh_codes) to avoid recursion.
513	        if any(
514	            slot_num not in slots_by_num or slots_by_num[slot_num].get("in_use") is None
515	            for slot_num in code_slots
516	        ):
517	            _LOGGER.debug(
518	                "Lock %s has missing/unknown slots, performing hard refresh",
519	                self.lock.entity_id,
520	            )
521	            await self._async_refresh_usercode_cache()
522	            slots = self._get_usercodes_from_cache()
523	            slots_by_num = {int(slot["code_slot"]): slot for slot in slots}
524	
525	        for slot in slots:
526	            code_slot = int(slot["code_slot"])
527	            usercode: str = slot["usercode"] or ""
528	            in_use: bool | None = slot["in_use"]
529	
530	            if not in_use:
531	                data[code_slot] = SlotCredential.empty()
532	            elif not usercode:
533	                # in_use but no code content (cache partially populated); skip
534	                continue
535	            elif usercode == "*" * len(usercode):
536	                # Masked code (all asterisks with slot in use)
537	                data[code_slot] = SlotCredential.unreadable()
538	            else:
539	                # Unmasked code
540	                data[code_slot] = SlotCredential.known(usercode)
541	
542	        slots_with_pin = [s for s, v in data.items() if v.is_present]
543	        slots_empty = [s for s, v in data.items() if v.is_empty]
544	        _LOGGER.debug(
545	            "Lock %s: %s slots with PIN %s, %s slots empty %s",
546	            self.lock.entity_id,
547	            len(slots_with_pin),
548	            slots_with_pin,
549	            len(slots_empty),
550	            slots_empty,
551	        )
552	        return data
553

147	
148	        Credentials are normally kept current by the access-control node-event
149	        push, but a missed or value-less event would otherwise strand a slot

159	    def _pin_state(self, data: str | bytes | None) -> SlotCredential:
160	        """Project Z-Wave credential data to a SlotCredential (readable when present)."""
161	        if not data:
162	            return SlotCredential.unreadable()
163	        # CredentialData.data is str | bytes; decode bytes so a Personal
164	        # Identification Number is the digit string, not "b'1234'".
165	        return SlotCredential.known(data if isinstance(data, str) else data.decode())
166	
167	    async def async_get_users(self) -> list[User]:
168	        """
169	        Read every user and all of their credentials from the lock.
170	
171	        Returns users carrying every credential type the domain model can
172	        represent (Personal Identification Number, Radio Frequency
173	        Identification, Near Field Communication, password, face,
174	        fingerprint). The base orchestration filters to Personal
175	        Identification Number at the slot-projection layer via
176	        ``user.pin_credentials``, so this method does no type-specific
177	        filtering -- direct callers see the full picture without an
178	        extra read. Z-Wave credential types with no domain equivalent
179	        (BLE, UWB, DESFIRE, unspecified/eye/hand biometrics) are
180	        dropped.
181	
182	        Uses the unified ``access_control`` API which dispatches to UC
183	        or U3C internally per node-zwave-js v15.23.4+. When that API is
184	        unusable for this lock (see ``async_get_capabilities``), users
185	        are synthesized from the User Code CC value DB instead: one
186	        implicit user per occupied slot with ``user_id == slot``,
187	        matching the User Code CC model where the user IS the credential.
188	        """
189	        if await self._async_uc_fallback_active():
190	            return await self._async_uc_users_from_value_db()
191	        try:
192	            users = await self.node.access_control.get_users_cached()
193	            credentials = await self.node.access_control.get_all_credentials_cached()
194	        except BaseZwaveJSServerError as err:
195	            raise LockDisconnected(f"get users failed: {err}") from err
196	        except HomeAssistantError as err:
197	            raise LockOperationFailed(f"get users failed: {err}") from err
198	        users_by_id: dict[int, User] = {
199	            user.user_id: User(
200	                user_id=user.user_id,
201	                name=user.user_name,
202	                active=user.active,
203	            )
204	            for user in users
205	        }
206	        for cred in credentials:
207	            domain_type = _ZWAVE_TO_DOMAIN_CREDENTIAL_TYPE.get(cred.type)
208	            owner = users_by_id.get(cred.user_id)
209	            if domain_type is None or owner is None:
210	                continue
211	            # _pin_state decodes the Personal Identification Number value
212	            # so the slot-projection sees a comparable string. For other
213	            # credential types the data is opaque to Lock Code Manager
214	            # (an RFID tag identifier, a biometric hash, ...), so they
215	            # surface as unreadable -- "the slot is occupied" without
216	            # revealing a value the integration would not act on anyway.
217	            state = (
218	                self._pin_state(cred.data)
219	                if cred.type is UserCredentialType.PIN_CODE
220	                else SlotCredential.unreadable()
221	            )
222	            owner.credentials.append(
223	                Credential(type=domain_type, slot=cred.slot, state=state)
224	            )
225	        return list(users_by_id.values())

227	    async def async_get_capabilities(self) -> LockCapabilities:
228	        """
229	        Report the lock's user/credential capabilities.
230	
231	        Auto-detects User Code CC (UC) vs User Credential CC (U3C) and
232	        returns capabilities shaped so the seam routes through the right
233	        code path.
234	
235	        The unified ``access_control`` API in node-zwave-js computes its
236	        capabilities from cached interview data; on some locks that data
237	        is degenerate -- the PIN credential type comes back either
238	        missing or advertising ``num_slots=0`` even though the lock
239	        manages codes fine through the legacy User Code CC (issue
240	        #1251, upstream fix in zwave-js/zwave-js#8873). When that
241	        happens AND the node actually advertises User Code CC, we fall
242	        back to reading the lock's UC slot count from the value DB and
243	        return slot-only capabilities: ``supports_user_management=False``
244	        and ``max_user_name_length=0``, which the seam recognizes as a
245	        slot-only lock and routes through the credential-only
246	        primitives (``async_set_credential`` / ``async_delete_credential``
247	        / ``async_get_users``) without the user lifecycle. All
248	        credential operations then use the User Code CC utilities
249	        directly. Once the upstream fix ships and the unified API
250	        reports usable PIN capabilities for these locks, the fallback
251	        detection stops triggering on its own.
252	        """
253	        try:
254	            caps = await lock_helpers.async_get_credential_capabilities(self.node)
255	        except BaseZwaveJSServerError as err:
256	            raise LockDisconnected(f"get capabilities failed: {err}") from err
257	        except HomeAssistantError as err:
258	            raise LockOperationFailed(f"get capabilities failed: {err}") from err
259	        pin = caps["supported_credential_types"].get(_PIN_TYPE_STR)
260	
261	        if pin and pin["num_slots"] > 0:
262	            # The unified API advertises real PIN credential slots.
263	            self._uc_fallback = False
264	            return LockCapabilities(
265	                supports_user_management=caps["supports_user_management"],
266	                max_users=caps["max_users"],
267	                credential_types={
268	                    CredentialType.PIN: CredentialTypeCapability(
269	                        num_slots=pin["num_slots"],
270	                        min_length=pin["min_length"],
271	                        max_length=pin["max_length"],
272	                        supports_learn=pin["supports_learn"],
273	                    )
274	                },
275	                max_user_name_length=caps.get("max_user_name_length", 0),
276	            )
277	
278	        # Degenerate unified capabilities (issue #1251): try the User Code
279	        # CC fallback layer; when it does not apply either, the lock has
280	        # no PIN support LCM can manage.
281	        if uc_caps := self._uc_fallback_capabilities():
282	            return uc_caps
283	        return LockCapabilities(
284	            supports_user_management=False,
285	            max_users=0,
286	            credential_types={},
287	            max_user_name_length=0,
288	        )

290	    async def async_set_user(self, user: User) -> SetUserResult:
291	        """
292	        Find-or-create the lock user for the LCM slot encoded in ``user.name``.
293	
294	        The base seam passes a tagged ``user.name`` (``lcm:<slot>:<display>``)
295	        whose slot is the LCM-side identity for this credential. The Z-Wave
296	        lock's own ``user_id`` is whatever Z-Wave happens to allocate; LCM
297	        no longer pins it to the slot. Discovery on every call:
298	
299	        1. Scan the lock's current user list for a user whose name carries
300	           the same ``lcm:<slot>:`` tag.
301	        2. If found (UPDATE): rename via ``async_set_user`` with the
302	           existing ``user_id``, return that id.
303	        3. If not (legacy adoption): scan for an *untagged* user whose
304	           ``user_id == slot`` that also owns a PIN at ``credential.slot
305	           == slot``. Pre-PR-C LCM pinned ``user_id`` to the slot, so
306	           such a user is almost certainly the LCM 2.0 user for this
307	           slot. Adopting it preserves a single per-slot anchor across
308	           the upgrade.
309	        4. Otherwise (CREATE): allocate a fresh ``user_id`` via
310	           ``async_set_user(user_id=None)`` (Z-Wave finds first free).
311	
312	        ``user.user_id`` set by the seam is used only as the slot identity
313	        when ``user.name`` is untagged (defensive fallback; the seam
314	        always passes a tagged name on this code path).
315	        """
316	        slot = self._slot_from_seam_user(user)
317	        existing_user_id = await self._find_user_index_for_slot(slot)
318	        write_user_id: int | None = existing_user_id
319	        try:
320	            result = await lock_helpers.async_set_user(
321	                self.node,
322	                user_id=write_user_id,
323	                user_name=user.name,
324	                active=user.active,
325	            )
326	        except BaseZwaveJSServerError as err:
327	            raise LockDisconnected(f"set user for slot {slot} failed: {err}") from err
328	        except HomeAssistantError as err:
329	            raise LockOperationFailed(
330	                f"set user for slot {slot} failed: {err}"
331	            ) from err
332	        return SetUserResult(
333	            user_id=result["user_id"], created=existing_user_id is None
334	        )
335	
336	    def _slot_from_seam_user(self, user: User) -> int:
337	        """
338	        Return the LCM slot encoded in ``user.name`` or fall back to ``user_id``.
339	
340	        The base seam always passes a tagged name; this helper centralizes
341	        the fallback so the rest of the provider can treat the resolved
342	        slot as a single value.
343	        """
344	        if user.name:
345	            slot, _ = parse_tag(user.name)
346	            if slot is not None:
347	                return slot
348	        return user.user_id
349	
350	    async def _find_user_index_for_slot(self, slot: int) -> int | None:
351	        """
352	        Return the lock ``user_id`` LCM owns for ``slot``, if any.
353	
354	        Two lookups, in priority order:
355	
356	        1. **Canonical** -- a user whose name carries the ``lcm:<slot>:``
357	           tag. This is the post-PR-C identity rule.
358	        2. **Legacy adoption** -- an *untagged* user whose ``user_id ==
359	           slot`` AND who owns a Personal Identification Number
360	           credential at ``credential.slot == slot``. Pre-PR-C LCM pinned
361	           ``user_id`` to the slot, so a user matching both halves of the
362	           old invariant is almost certainly the LCM 2.0 user for this
363	           slot. Adopting it (the subsequent ``async_set_user`` rewrites
364	           the name to the tagged form) preserves a single identifiable
365	           user per slot across the upgrade. Without this fallback the
366	           new model would CREATE a second user every time, silently
367	           leaving the pre-upgrade Personal Identification Number active
368	           on the lock.
369	
370	           The legacy pass MUST skip users whose names already parse to
371	           ANY LCM slot, so a user tagged for slot A is never adopted as
372	           slot B's anchor.
373	
374	        Returns ``None`` when neither lookup matches.
375	        """
376	        users = await self.async_get_users()
377	        try:
378	            return next(
379	                existing.user_id
380	                for existing in users
381	                if existing.name and parse_tag(existing.name)[0] == slot
382	            )
383	        except StopIteration:
384	            return next(
385	                (
386	                    existing.user_id
387	                    for existing in users
388	                    if existing.user_id == slot
389	                    and parse_tag(existing.name or "")[0] is None
390	                    for cred in existing.pin_credentials
391	                    if cred.slot == slot
392	                ),
393	                None,
394	            )
395

397	        """Delete the lock user (cascades its credentials)."""
398	        try:
399	            await lock_helpers.async_delete_user(self.node, user_id)
400	        except BaseZwaveJSServerError as err:
401	            raise LockDisconnected(f"delete user {user_id} failed: {err}") from err
402	        except HomeAssistantError as err:
403	            raise LockOperationFailed(f"delete user {user_id} failed: {err}") from err
